Abstract: Despite their potential in standardized graph tasks, Large Language Models (LLMs) remain brittle to real-world shifts in node identifiers and task formulation. While deterministic graph tools are invariant to such shifts, extracting topological structures from noisy text is highly fragile for LLMs, which often overfit to surface patterns. Moreover, mitigating these parsing failures via multi-agent systems incurs prohibitive latency. To address this, we propose GRAIN, a single-agent framework optimized via reinforcement learning. GRAIN models reasoning as a semantic parsing and tool-execution pipeline, guided by a Structure Invariance Reward. By validating extracted intermediate graphs against ground-truth topologies, this reward forces the LLM to learn robust text-to-structure mappings rather than memorizing linguistic artifacts. We also introduce GRIT, a benchmark evaluating sensitivity to such linguistic shifts. GRAIN outperforms multi-agent baselines by 16.45\% in accuracy with approximately 24\% lower latency. Furthermore, it demonstrates superior structural generalization, halving the out-of-distribution (OOD) gap of SFT models (from 15.77\% to 7.80\%) and maintaining robustness on large-scale graphs beyond the training distribution.

</details>


### [83] [Feature Transformation Enhanced Jacobi Polynomial Graph Filtering for Graph Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27144)
*Xiang Wang,Zhijun Cheng,Zhenyu Meng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种名为JPGFN的新型图异常检测方法，通过特征分离变换网络（FSTNN）学习细粒度节点特征、基于雅可比多项式的自适应图滤波模块捕捉复杂频域特征、以及节点标签约束模块充分挖掘标签信息，在多个真实数据集上显著优于主流方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于频域滤波的图异常检测方法存在三大挑战：(1) 使用静态基函数构建图滤波器，无法自适应地匹配图数据的频域分布；(2) 未充分考虑节点特征向量中各属性的重要性，导致细粒度信息丢失；(3) 节点标签信息利用不充分，影响检测性能。

Method: 提出JPGFN方法，包含三个核心模块：(1) 特征分离变换网络（FSTNN），通过特征分离并对节点特征的不同维度施加非线性变换，以更好地学习细粒度节点特征；(2) 基于雅可比多项式的自适应图滤波模块，用于自适应捕获图信号的复杂频域特征；(3) 节点标签约束模块，用于强化节点标签的利用并提升图异常检测性能。

Result: 在多个真实世界数据集上的实验结果表明，所提出的JPGFN方法在性能上显著优于主流图异常检测方法。

Conclusion: 通过自适应频域滤波与特征变换增强相结合，并引入标签约束，JPGFN有效解决了现有频域滤波GAD方法在滤波器适应性、细粒度特征利用和标签信息挖掘方面的不足，展现了优越的检测性能。

Abstract: In recent years, graph anomaly detection (GAD) based on frequency-domain filtering have achieved promising results. However, existing approaches still face three major challenges: First, they use static basic function to constructed graph filter which cannot effectively adapt to the frequency-domain distribution of graph data. Second, they fail to adequately consider the importance information of each attribute in the node feature vector, leading to the loss of fine-grained information. Third, they insufficiently utilize node labels for GAD. To address these issues, this paper proposes a novel graph anomaly detection method called JPGFN (Feature Transformation Enhanced Jacobi Polynomial Graph Filtering Network). First, a Feature Separation Transformation Network (FSTNN) is developed to better learn fine-grained node features by feature separation and applying nonlinear transformations to node features across different dimensions. Second, an adaptive Jacobi polynomial graph filtering module is constructed based on Jacobi polynomials to adaptively capture complex frequency-domain features of graph signals. Finally, a node label constraint module is developed to facilitate the use of node labels and enhance the performance of GAD. Experimental results on multiple real-world datasets demonstrate that the proposed method significantly outperforms mainstream approaches.

</details>


### [84] [Thomson: Continual Learning of Frontier Models for SovereignAI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27147)
*Shengzhuang Chen,Jerrod Parker,Yejin Bang,Andrew M. Bean,Nabeel Seedat,Stefan Winzeck,Daniil Glazko,Jannik Zgraggen,Fangyi Yu,Scott Arnott,Dietrich Trautmann,Luca Ciuffreda,Guglielmo Bonifazi,Davide Romano,Bradley Bell,Kirsty Fielding,Daniele Giofrè,Tom Zielund,Ipshita Chatterjee,Sneha Murthy Ghantasala,Manpreet Nanreh,John Scoville,Maciej Sakowicz,Wassim Seifeddine,Lukas Thede,Jonathan Richard Schwarz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出通过持续学习（Continual Learning）在开源权重模型上实现前沿模型性能的路径，论证主权AI（SovereignAI）可以由更广泛的机构实现。作者以Thomson模型为例，展示了在中等预算下通过现代中/后训练栈的精心干预，可获得与多代模型迭代相当的性能提升，同时消除遗忘问题，并在智能体任务、安全、法律、税务、多语言及深度研究等领域与前沿模型竞争。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿模型开发被少数资金雄厚的机构垄断，造成开发者与用户之间的信息、经济和权力不对称。虽然公共讨论呼吁主权AI（组织独立构建、部署和治理AI的能力），但缺乏在多样化资金条件下短期内实现这一目标的具体方案。

Method: 采用持续学习策略，基于现有开源权重模型进行训练，利用现代中训练与后训练技术栈（mid- & post-training stack），同时引入保护可塑性与稳定性的安全机制，对参数施加少量高影响力的干预。这与小规模微调、提示工程或工具增强冻结模型等受限方法形成对比。

Result: 训练出的Thomson模型在智能体任务、安全、法律、税务、多语言及大规模深度研究等领域与近期前沿模型表现相当。评估显示独特的π形能力模式：广泛能力（包括未明确目标的能力）均有显著提升，同时几乎完全消除了窄域领域适应中常见的遗忘问题。性能提升可媲美多代模型迭代的累积收益，而计算和人员预算远低于普遍预期。

Conclusion: 前沿性能并非大型科技公司的专属，通过持续学习开源权重模型，更广泛的机构可以在中等预算下拥有SovereignAI栈的大部分组件（模型、工具基础设施、价值观与数据隐私），从而打破前沿AI开发的信息、经济和权力不对称。

Abstract: The development of frontier models is commonly perceived to be the exclusive remit of a small number of heavily funded players, creating an information, economic and power asymmetry between developers and the diverse user base of modern AI. Recent public discourse acknowledges this concern, calling for SovereignAI (an organisation's capability to independently build, deploy and govern AI use), but offers little concrete advice on how this can be achieved in the short term under a diversity of funding settings. We argue that frontier performance is achievable by a wide range of institutions through Continual Learning on readily available open-weight models. Unlike limited approaches such as small-scale fine-tuning, prompt engineering, or tool-augmentation of a frozen model, our approach exploits a modern mid- & post-training stack while introducing safeguards that preserve both plasticity and stability at each stage, making the minimal number of high-impact interventions on the parameters. This yields gains comparable to those typically seen across multiple successive model generations, at compute and personnel budgets substantially lower than commonly thought, making ownership of large parts of the SovereignAI stack (model, tool infrastructure, values & data privacy) viable for far more actors. We demonstrate this with Thomson, a general-purpose frontier model trained with an enhanced focus on high-stakes professional work. Thomson performs competitively with recent frontier models across agentic tasks, safety, legal, tax & multilingualism, and large-scale Deep Research. Evaluations show a distinctive $π$-shaped pattern: distinct improvements across a wide range of capabilities, including those not explicitly targeted, while almost completely eliminating the forgetting problem common to narrow domain adaptation.

</details>


### [85] [BPMN4CAI: A BPMN Extension for Modeling Dynamic Conversational AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27149)
*Björn-Lennart Eger,Daniel Rose,Barbara Dinter*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种符合标准的BPMN扩展（BPMN4CAI），用于在业务流程中表示动态、上下文敏感的对话式AI交互，并通过案例研究验证了其适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对话式AI系统（如聊天机器人和虚拟助手）在数字业务流程中日益重要，但现有的BPMN标准在表示动态、上下文敏感的交互方面存在局限性，存在方法论和实践上的研究空白。

Method: 采用设计科学研究（Design Science Research）方法论，系统地扩展现有BPMN元素并融入专门化组件，开发出标准合规的BPMN4CAI扩展框架。

Result: 通过案例研究验证了BPMN4CAI框架的适用性和相关性，结果表明该扩展能够促进流程中的适应性决策、稳健的上下文管理以及透明交互。

Conclusion: BPMN4CAI扩展有效弥合了BPMN标准与对话式AI需求之间的鸿沟，为在业务流程中建模和集成对话式AI提供了可行且标准合规的解决方案。

Abstract: Conversational AI systems, such as chatbots and virtual assistants, are becoming increasingly important to digital business processes. However, the established Business Process Model and Notation (BPMN) standard faces challenges when representing dynamic, context-sensitive interactions. This paper addresses this methodological and practical research gap by developing a standard-compliant BPMN extension (BPMN4CAI). Using Design Science Research methodology, this paper develops an approach that systematically extends existing BPMN elements and incorporates specialized components. The applicability and relevance of the BPMN4CAI framework are demonstrated and evaluated through a case study. The results show that the BPMN4CAI extension facilitates adaptive decision-making processes, robust context management, and transparent interactions for Conversational AI within business processes.

</details>


### [86] [Calibrated Enough to Know, Not Calibrated to Act: Fabricated Evidence Makes LLM Agents Commit to the Unknowable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27167)
*Pranav Aggarwal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现，面对专业外观的市场面板，12个前沿LLM智能体对不可预测问题做出方向性判断的比例从6.5%飙升至54.0%；即使面板数据完全造假，承诺率仍接近真实数据水平。作者定位出失败关键在于"行动/不行动"门控机制而非能力或信念，并证明该门控可通过540个合成案例的监督微调在3B模型中降至0.0%，且能迁移到多个未见领域，但该门控对响应格式极为敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究LLM在专业外观证据面前为何会对本质上不可预测的问题做出信心十足的方向性判断（"过度信任包装权威"现象），并定位这一失效究竟源于能力缺陷、信念偏差、判断缺失，还是行动门控机制的故障——最终目标是理解该缺陷的可训练性与可修复性。

Method: 跨12个前沿模型设置多个实验梯度：从裸问题到逐步升级的专业市场面板（包含完全伪造数据的对照组）；测量承诺率、陈述概率、可知道性分类；随后对3B模型进行540个合成案例（骰子、硬币、罐子、计时器）的监督微调训练行动门控，并测试其对原始案例及三个未见领域的迁移，同时检验不同响应格式（自由推理vs刚性格式）的影响。

Result: ①承诺率随证据升级从6.5%升至54.0%；伪造面板仍使承诺率从24.5%升至36.8%，与真实数据（37.6%）统计无差异。②模型在可回答问题上几乎总是正确作答，能力无缺陷；陈述概率几乎不随梯度变动且劣于气候基线；模型90%能识别问题不可知但仅0.4%因此拒绝行动。③门控故障集中在少数模型而非普遍现象。④3B模型经540例微调后承诺率降至0.0%并迁移至三个未见领域，但仅在可自由推理的格式下成立；刚性格式下模型仍会自信犯错。

Conclusion: 决定模型是否采取行动的关键不是信息量，而是信息的"包装权威"；失败可精确定位到"行动/不行动"门控环节，该门控可训练但高度依赖上下文格式——部署时须同时理解其可训练性和情境脆弱性两方面特性。

Abstract: An LLM agent shown a professional-looking market panel commits to a directional call on a provably unpredictable question far more often than one asked the bare question: across 12 frontier models, commitment rises from 6.5% to 54.0% as evidence is escalated. It commits just as readily when every number on the panel is invented: fabricating the entire display, so nothing the model can see is true except the question itself, still lifts commitment from 24.5% to 36.8%, statistically indistinguishable from the 37.6% produced by genuine market data. What unlocks confident action is not information but the authority of its packaging. The failure is narrow and locatable. Incapacity is not the answer: on matched answerable questions attached to the same panels, the same models answer essentially always, at near-perfect accuracy. Nor is it belief - stated probabilities barely move across the gradient that swings action by 48 points, and score worse than a climatological baseline. Missing judgment isn't it either: asked to classify a question's knowability before acting, models call it irreducible 90% of the time and then commit on just 0.4% of those. The act/don't-act gate is what fails, and the effect is concentrated in a few models rather than universal. Because the gate is separable, it can be trained. Supervised fine-tuning of a 3B model on 540 synthetic cases, predominantly dice, coins, jars and timers, drives commitment to 0.0% on the original cases and transfers to three unseen domains. It does not survive everything: the gate holds exactly when the response format leaves room to reason, and rigid formats that remove that room leave the model confident and wrong on questions it otherwise answers correctly. The gate is trainable and context-fragile, and deployment needs both halves of that sentence.

</details>


### [87] [What Makes Good Agentic Data? An ACE Lens on Data Generation for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27260)
*Xingshan Zeng,Zishan Xu,Boju Zhang,Yuzhou Wu,Lingzhi Wang,Jianghao Lin,Liangyou Li,Yasheng Wang,Lifeng Shang,Xin Jiang,Weinan Zhang,Yong Yu,Qun Liu,Weiwen Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个用于智能体（LLM agent）数据生成领域的双层分析框架：第一层将智能体数据表示为因子化对象(E,q,τ,v)，即环境规范、任务信号、交互实现和可选验证器；第二层通过ACE（准确性-复杂性-多样性）视角将生成形式化为约束分布设计。文章系统梳理了现有工作在验证、难度构建和覆盖率扩展方面的进展，指出领域正朝向执行锚定的准确性、学习者相对的复杂性和超越表面变化的多样性发展，核心挑战不是生成更多数据，而是在智能体与环境演化中持续分配有效、信息丰富且不冗余的经验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型智能体日益依赖生成的交互数据来学习与外部环境交互，但现有研究按领域分散组织、评估标准异构，导致难以识别各领域共通的生成机制，并常将候选构建与验证/选择混为一谈。因此需要统一框架来组织和分析智能体数据生成领域。

Method: 提出两层框架：(1) 将智能体数据统一表示为四元组(E,q,τ,v)，按主锚点和依赖结构对生成范式分类；(2) 通过ACE（Accuracy-Complexity-divErsity）透镜将生成形式化为约束分布设计——准确性确立可行支持集，复杂性根据学习者能力分配学习质量，多样性控制数据覆盖与冗余。基于此框架回顾现有工作在验证、难度校准和行为覆盖方面的做法。

Result: 文献分析揭示三大趋势：向执行锚定的准确性（execution-grounded accuracy）转变、向学习者相对的复杂性（learner-relative complexity）转变、以及超越表面变化或数据集规模的多样性追求（diversity beyond surface variation or dataset size）。文章进一步讨论了这些趋势对缩放、数据来源、训练范式和自适应学习的启示。

Conclusion: 智能体数据生成的核心挑战不在于扩充数据量，而在于随着智能体和环境不断演化，持续分配有效（valid）、信息丰富（informative）且不冗余（non-redundant）的经验。ACE框架为这一领域的系统化发展和未来研究方向提供了统一的组织视角。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly rely on generated interaction data to learn how to interact with external environments. Agentic data generation must maintain consistency among environments, tasks, interactions, and success signals while producing experience that is useful rather than merely abundant. Existing work spans many agent domains, but domain-centered organization and heterogeneous evaluation often obscure common generation mechanisms and conflate candidate construction with verification and selection. This work develops a two-level framework for the field. First, we represent agentic data as a common factorized object $(E,q,τ,v)$, comprising an environment specification, task signal, interaction realization, and optional verifier. We organize generation paradigms by their primary anchor and dependency structure. Second, we formulate generation as constrained distribution design through the Accuracy-Complexity-divErsity (ACE) lens. Accuracy establishes the feasible support of grounded and internally consistent data. Within this support, Complexity places learning mass relative to the capability of a declared learner and execution configuration, while divErsity controls coverage and redundancy of data. Using this framework, we explore how prior work verifies generated experience, constructs and calibrates difficulty, and expands behavioral coverage. The literature reveals a shift toward execution-grounded accuracy, learner-relative complexity, and diversity beyond surface variation or dataset size. We further discuss broader directions and emerging trends in agentic data generation through the ACE lens, including their implications for scaling, data sources, training regimes and adaptive learning. Overall, the central challenge is not simply to generate more data, but to continually allocate valid, informative, and non-redundant experience as agents and environments evolve.

</details>


### [88] [Naive Prompt Optimization: Rethinking the Need for Complex Prompt Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27266)
*Yuan Chang,Xiaoqi Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Naive Prompt Optimization (NPO)，一种轻量级单谱系提示优化方法，通过教师模型迭代修订提示并利用rollout反馈，在不需要复杂搜索过程的情况下，以更少的rollout数量达到或超越复杂优化器（如GEPA）的性能，且教师模型越强优势越明显，优化后的提示还能跨模型迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者观察到近年来的提示优化器设计日益复杂，但复杂性未必带来相应收益。出于加速智能体递归自改进（RSI）的目标，作者希望探索简单方法是否足以媲美复杂优化器，从而降低计算成本并推动更高效的自主智能体发展。

Method: NPO采用轻量级单谱系（single-lineage）迭代策略，使用一个教师模型作为优化器，结合rollout反馈反复修订提示词。整个过程是线性的：每次迭代仅沿一条演化路径修正提示，不涉及复杂的种群搜索或多路径并行探索。

Result: NPO在相同或更少的rollout数量下达到或优于GEPA的性能；教师模型越强，NPO的优势越大，表明强教师推理能力可部分替代优化器端的搜索复杂性。在交互式游戏中NPO与GEPA整体表现相当，但GRPO在某些不易于提示优化的任务上表现更好。此外，NPO优化出的提示在应用于其他学生模型（尤其同族模型）时仍能带来类似的性能提升。

Conclusion: 初步结果表明，简单、线性的提示优化方法可以媲美甚至超越显著更复杂的搜索过程，提示优化器的设计不应盲目追求复杂性，强教师模型的能力本身就能在简单框架内释放足够的优化潜力。

Abstract: Efficiently improving autonomous agents across diverse tasks is central to accelerating recursive self-improvement (RSI) in agentic AI, with prompt optimization emerging as a promising approach capable of delivering performance gains comparable to those achieved by fine-tuning model weights, while reducing computational costs in both optimization and serving. However, recent developments increasingly favor unnecessarily complex prompt optimizers. We introduce Naive Prompt Optimization (NPO), a lightweight single-lineage method that iteratively revises prompts using a teacher model with rollout feedback. NPO achieves comparable or better performance than GEPA with fewer rollouts, and its advantage increases with stronger teacher models, suggesting that stronger teacher reasoning can partially substitute for optimizer-side search complexity. In interactive games, NPO remains broadly competitive with GEPA, while GRPO performs better on some tasks less amenable to prompt optimization. We also show that NPO-optimized prompts elicit similar performance improvements when applied verbatim to other student models, especially across models within the same family. Overall, our preliminary results show that simple, linear prompt optimization can rival substantially more sophisticated and complex search procedures.

</details>


### [89] [BrailleBench: Investigating Multi-Criteria Braille Comprehension in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27268)
*Jinghan Zhang,Fengran Mo,Zhiyu Chen,Xiaoyan Han,Kunpeng Liu,Chang-Tien Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出BrailleBench基准，用于评估大型语言模型在盲文理解方面的能力，涵盖数学、常识和多跳问答等任务，跨越英语及1级和2级盲文。通过确定性、专家审核的流程构建5,570个实例，评估六个代表性LLM后发现，模型在盲文能力上与印刷英语存在显著差距，且盲文理解和表达呈不对称性，2级盲文在输入侧尤为脆弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM为知识获取和计算辅助提供中介，但其能力应同样惠及弱势群体。盲人和聋盲用户需要通过盲文（其指示符、缩写和数字表示对模型理解提出独特要求）获得相同功能，但现有AI系统对这些用户的包容性尚不清楚，缺乏系统性的盲文理解评估基准。

Method: 构建BrailleBench基准：从五个数据集中对齐5,570个实例，涵盖数学、常识和多跳问答，跨英语及盲文1级和2级。设计不同配置以测试系统理解盲文内容、用盲文表达答案以及端到端盲文交互的能力。通过自建Braille Toolkit进行确定性、专家审核的流水线构建，避免使用LLM生成数据以防止评估偏差。评估六个代表性LLM。

Result: 实验结果显示印刷英语能力与盲文可访问性之间存在持续差距；盲文理解与表达呈现不对称性，2级盲文在输入侧比1级盲文更脆弱；完全使用盲文的请求进一步降低模型性能。

Conclusion: 实验观察为未来盲文AI系统的发展提供了有价值的指导，BrailleBench的所有相关资源均公开供未来研究使用。

Abstract: Although Large language models (LLMs) mediate access to knowledge and computational assistance, their capabilities should benefit vulnerable groups in the same way. However, it is unclear whether existing AI systems are inclusive enough for blind and deafblind users to access the same functionality through Braille, whose indicators, contractions, and digital representations introduce distinct requirements for model comprehension. To this end, we introduce BrailleBench, a benchmark for evaluating LLMs in Braille comprehension from different Criteria. BrailleBench aligns 5,570 instances from five datasets, including mathematics, commonsense, and multi-hop question answering across English and Braille Grades 1 and 2. Different configurations are designed to understand whether the systems can comprehend Braille-authored content, express answers in Braille, and complete end-to-end Braille interaction. To ensure the quality and prevent evaluation bias, the benchmark is built through a deterministic, expert-reviewed pipeline via a self-created Braille Toolkit without using any data instances generated by LLMs. We evaluate six representative LLMs from various aspects. The results reveal a persistent gap between print-English capability and Braille accessibility. Braille understanding and expression are asymmetric, where Grade 2 is especially fragile on the input side compared to Grade 1, and fully Braille requests further reduce performance. The experimental observations provide valuable guidance for the development of future Braille AI systems. All related resources in BrailleBench are publicly available for future research.

</details>


### [90] [LLMs Can Design Near-Optimal OR Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27296)
*Jackie Baek*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了大型语言模型（LLM）是否能为特定的运筹学（OR）问题设计有效算法。研究覆盖库存控制、排队网络控制和品类优化三类问题，在两个层面上评估LLM能力：级别1模型直接为单实例提供解决方案，级别2模型仅根据问题类别描述和参数范围生成通用算法。最强的gpt-5.6-sol模型在几乎所有测试实例上达到或超越了现有最佳方法，且即使在级别2（算法在见到评估实例前已固定）也表现优异。此外，间隔不到8个月发布的模型之间性能提升显著，表明LLM在该领域的算法设计能力正在快速进步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验前沿LLM是否能作为运筹学算法设计的严肃经验基线，填补评估LLM在特定OR问题上自动生成算法能力的空白，并为OR社区提供新的算法设计范式。

Method: 在库存控制、排队网络控制和品类优化三类问题中，设计两个评估层面：级别1给定单实例返回解决方案，级别2仅给定问题类别描述和参数范围返回通用算法。使用单一未调优的提示词描述问题，模型通过Python沙箱工具在固定计算预算内交互求解。对比最强模型与现有专门方法的性能。

Result: 最强模型gpt-5.6-sol在几乎所有评估实例上匹配或优于现有最佳方法；级别2中返回的算法在未见评估实例前已固定，仍表现优异；不同代次模型（发布间隔不到8个月）性能显著提升，表明该能力快速演进。

Conclusion: 对于所研究的特定OR问题，单次未调优的LLM查询即可生成与专门方法竞争力相当的算法，前沿LLM可被视为OR问题算法设计的严肃经验基线。

Abstract: We ask whether large language models (LLMs) can design effective algorithms for well-specified operations research (OR) problems. We study inventory control, queueing network control, and assortment optimization. We evaluate two levels of LLM use: at level 1, the model receives one problem instance and returns a solution for that instance; at level 2, it receives only the problem class description and broad parameter ranges, and returns an algorithm that maps instance parameters to solutions. Human input is minimal: we give one untuned prompt that describes the problem, and the model has access to a Python sandbox tool with a fixed compute budget.
  The strongest model we test, gpt-5.6-sol, matches or outperforms the best existing method on almost all evaluated instances. This holds even at level 2, where the returned algorithm is fixed before seeing the evaluation instances. Performance also improves sharply across models released less than eight months apart, suggesting that this capability is moving quickly. Thus, for the well-specified operations problems we study, a single untuned LLM query can already produce algorithms competitive with specialized methods. These results suggest that frontier LLMs can be a serious empirical baseline for algorithm design in well-specified OR problems.

</details>


### [91] [Verify Smarter, Evolve Further: Efficient Harness Evolution through Behavior-Aware Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27311)
*Jinghan Xu,Yikai Zhang,Aili Chen,Weiyuan Li,Jiaqing Liang,Deqing Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出HarnessLens，一种面向语言模型代理（agent）的预算感知框架，用于自动化harness进化。该框架联合探索任务空间与用户可配置组件，从执行轨迹中推导候选修改，并通过可归因证据门控（attributable-evidence gate）在行为相关任务上选择性验证每个候选。在三个agent harness和四个基准上，平均持出性能提升7.6-13.6%，同时显著减少评估预算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的propose-and-verify方法通常在固定任务集上对每个候选打分，一方面浪费了大量rollout在无关行为上，另一方面聚合分数会掩盖特定回归问题。而修改agent harness需要昂贵的验证成本，因此需要一个更高效、行为感知的验证机制。

Method: HarnessLens包含三个核心环节：（1）联合探索任务空间与用户可配置组件；（2）从执行轨迹中推导候选harness修改方案；（3）引入可归因证据门控，仅在行为相关的任务上选择性验证候选，实现预算感知的验证。

Result: 在三个agent harness和四个基准上，HarnessLens将平均持出性能提升7.6-13.6%，同时比竞争的baseline消耗更少的评估预算，展现出更好的样本效率。

Conclusion: 带有显式归因的行为感知验证（behavior-aware verification）能够在受限交互预算下实现更可靠、更样本高效的harness进化，为自动化代理系统优化提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Agent harnesses shape how language-model agents use instructions, tools, and runtime components, but adapting these harnesses requires costly verification. Existing propose-and-verify methods typically score every candidate on a fixed task set, wasting rollouts on unrelated behaviors and allowing aggregate scores to obscure specific regressions. We introduce HarnessLens, a budget-aware framework for automated harness evolution. HarnessLens jointly explores the task space and user-configurable components, derives candidate modifications from execution trajectories, and selectively verifies each candidate on behavior-relevant tasks using an attributable-evidence gate. Across three agent harnesses and four benchmarks, HarnessLens improves average held-out performance by 7.6-13.6% while consuming substantially less evaluation budget than competing baselines. These results demonstrate that behavior-aware verification with explicit attribution enables more reliable and sample-efficient harness evolution under constrained interaction budgets. Our code is available at https://github.com/jhxu5214/HarnessLens.

</details>


### [92] [Not All Eval-Awareness Is Equal: Capabilities Framing Predicts Compliance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27340)
*Allison Zhuang,Santiago Aranguri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示评估感知（eval-awareness）并非单一同质变量，而可细分为能力倾向（capabilities-flavored）与安全倾向（safety-flavored）等不同框架，这些框架对模型合规行为的预测截然不同，且抑制评估感知的干预效果取决于具体抑制的是哪种成分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全评估管线中常将评估感知视为单一数量进行抑制，但这一假设可能过于简化，导致安全评估结果失真或误导。作者旨在解构评估感知的内在结构，说明其并非行为上统一的量。

Method: 在Qwen3-32B模型上使用FORTRESS数据集，通过分析思维链（chain-of-thought）中言语化的评估感知，将其分类为能力倾向、安全倾向、两者兼具或两者皆非；随后测试不同框架对合规性的预测差异，并通过CoT预填充（prefill）干预在评估感知负向输出上进行因果验证。

Result: 能力倾向框架在合规性预测上比安全倾向框架高出+24到+46个百分点（跨所有测试的steering条件）。CoT预填充干预中10/11个prefill将合规行为朝预测方向转移，支持因果性。聚合抑制率可以变动而安全相关成分不变，相同的"X%抑制"可对应质上不同的行为结果。

Conclusion: 评估感知在行为上并非统一量：不应以单一聚合指标来衡量或抑制，安全评估管线需要区分其能力倾向与安全倾向成分，以避免无效甚至适得其反的干预。

Abstract: Steering interventions targeting eval-awareness, a model's recognition that it is being tested, are increasingly used in safety evaluation pipelines, where evaluation-awareness is treated as a single quantity to be suppressed. We show that verbalized eval-awareness in chain-of-thought can be identified as capabilities-flavored ("the user is testing my ability to follow instructions"), safety-flavored ("the user is testing my boundaries"), both, or neither: framings that predict compliance very differently. On Qwen3-32B over the FORTRESS dataset, capabilities-framing predicts compliance with a +24 to +46 percentage-point gap over safety-framing across all tested steering conditions. A CoT-prefill intervention on eval-awareness-negative rollouts suggests the link is causal, with 10 of 11 prefills shifting compliance in the predicted direction. Then, eval-awareness is not behaviorally uniform: aggregate suppression rates can move while the safety-relevant component does not, and the same "X% suppression of eval-awareness" can correspond to qualitatively different behavioral outcomes.

</details>


### [93] [Sophistication in GenAI Use: Field Evidence from a Large Firm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27364)
*Nicholas J. Hallman,Zachary T. Kowaleski,Anu Puvvada,Jaime J. Schmidt*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了某大型企业后台部门约4000名员工在2025年八个月期间生成式AI使用的精细化程度，分析了713,564条员工提示词及其对应的大语言模型响应。研究发现：资深员工展现出更精细化的genAI使用；精细化程度因职能而异，在战略、数字创新和项目管理三类职能中最突出；且精细化程度既不随时间改善，也不因正式AI培训而产生持久提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着生成式AI在企业中的大规模普及，如何衡量和提升员工使用genAI的精细化程度成为管理层和研究者共同关注的重要问题。以往研究缺乏大规模、细粒度的工作场所实证数据，难以系统刻画员工与genAI交互的熟练度差异及其决定因素。

Method: 使用某大型企业的专有数据，覆盖15个职能领域的近4000名后台员工，收集2025年八个月期间713,564条员工提示词及对应的大语言模型响应。对员工的genAI使用精细化程度进行量化度量，并进行跨层级（资历）、跨职能及时间维度、培训效果三个维度的比较分析。

Result: 三大主要发现：（1）资深员工展现出更高的genAI使用精细化程度，支持领域专长与genAI能力互补的观点；（2）精细化程度在职能间差异显著，战略、数字创新和项目管理三类职能最高，三者共同聚焦于全公司战略举措与组织变革；（3）精细化程度随时间无改善，且正式AI培训未带来持久提升，提示精细化使用习惯较难改变。

Conclusion: 研究提供了关于精细化genAI使用的度量和洞见，可供管理者用于改善员工使用genAI的实际效果，也可供研究者作为未来研究的基础。

Abstract: We study how sophistication in generative AI (genAI) use varies among the back-office workforce of a large firm. Using proprietary data, we observe 713,564 employee prompts and their corresponding large language model responses from nearly 4,000 back-office employees across 15 functional areas over eight months in 2025. We document three main findings. First, senior employees exhibit more sophisticated genAI use, consistent with domain expertise complementing genAI capabilities. Second, sophistication varies considerably across functions and is highest in Strategy, Digital Innovation, and Project Management, three groups that share a focus on firmwide strategic initiatives and organizational change. Third, we observe neither improvements in sophistication over time nor lasting improvements following formal AI training, suggesting that sophisticated use can be difficult to change. Together, our study provides measures of and insights into sophisticated genAI use that managers can use to improve outcomes and that researchers can use in future research.

</details>


### [94] [Learning a Continuous Sepsis Severity Score Without Hour-by-Hour Supervision: A Two-Site Retrospective Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27421)
*Kevin Zhu,Ryan Zhang,Baraa Abed,Tilendra Choudhary,Malvern Madondo,Mehak Arora,Yixuan Yang,Alasdair Gent,Aditya Nagori,Omer T. Inan,Krista L. Haines,Patrick Georgoff,Suresh M. Agarwal,Vijay Krishnamoorthy,Tetsu Ohnuma,Mihai V. Podgoreanu,Michael R. Pinsky,Gilles Clermont,Craig M. Coopersmith,Craig S. Jabaley,Rishikesan Kamaleswaran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Sepsis-3标准、利用43项常规记录变量在72小时治疗窗口内通过患者轨迹学习的新型脓毒症严重程度指数。与传统基于固定变量的SOFA等指标不同，该指数以死亡率为治疗级别的排序信号而非逐状态目标，创新性地实现了跨时间步的非均匀信用分配。研究基于两个医院系统共36,807名患者，结果显示非幸存者比幸存者得分高1.19-1.64分（0-10分制），与乳酸变化相关（rho=0.39），跨机构一致性良好，具有作为临床决策辅助工具的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的脓毒症严重程度指标（如SOFA）基于几十年前确定的固定变量和权重，离散化粗糙且校准队列已不符合当代重症医学实践。目前没有从患者轨迹中直接学习得到的替代指标在常规临床使用。这促使研究者开发一种直接从患者数据轨迹学习的新型脓毒症指数。

Method: 研究采用回顾性双队列设计，包括麻萨诸塞州和佐治亚州两个医院系统的29,116名和7,691名符合Sepsis-3标准的成年患者。使用43项常规记录变量，定义了72小时治疗窗口。关键创新在于使用死亡率作为治疗级别（treatment-level）的排序信号而非逐状态目标，允许信用在时间步间非均匀地重新分配。评估采用永久性20%测试保留集、临床场景描述和Spearman相关性分析，不确定性区间通过自举重采样整患者获得。同时评估了跨机构一致性和外部验证。

Result: 在0-10分制下，非幸存者比幸存者的得分在所有基线SOFA-2分层内高出1.19-1.64分，在乳酸、平均动脉压（MAP）和肌酐分层内也有类似结果。指数随时间的患者内变化与乳酸变化相关（Spearman rho=0.39；n=1,854），与MAP和肌酐的相关性较弱但类似。跨机构一致性为同站点相关性的70-77%，外部患者内相关性为0.54和0.59（上限分别为0.92和0.90）。指数与既有指标相关，而无效控制在零附近。

Conclusion: 该脓毒症指数提供了每小时级别的预后信息，能够有意义地区分患者结局且与临床预期一致，显示出作为补充临床判断的决策支持工具的潜力。

Abstract: Currently used sepsis severity indices rely on fixed variables and weights established decades ago, which are coarsely discretized and calibrated to a cohort that no longer reflects contemporary critical care. No alternative learned directly from patient trajectories is in routine use. We conducted a retrospective two-cohort study on a total of 29,116 and 7,691 adult patients meeting Sepsis-3 criteria from two hospital systems in Massachusetts and Georgie, respectively.
  We developed a sepsis index using 43 routinely charted variables over a 72-hour treatment window. Unlike previous studies, we use mortality as a treatment-level ranking signal rather than a per-state target, allowing credit to be redistributed non-uniformly across timesteps. Evaluation was done on a permanent 20% test holdout, using clinical vignettes and Spearman correlation. Uncertainty intervals were obtained by bootstrap resampling of whole patients. Under this ranking scheme, non-survivors scored 1.19-1.64 points higher than survivors on a 0-10 scale within all strata of baseline SOFA-2, with similar results stratifying within lactate, mean arterial pressure (MAP), and creatinine. Within-patient change in the index correlated with change in lactate (Spearman rho = 0.39; n = 1,854). Similar, weaker correlations were found for MAP and creatinine. On a cohort level, cross-institutional agreement measured by Spearman correlation between models trained on different sites, were 70-77% of same-site correlation. External within-patient correlations were 0.54 and 0.59 against ceilings of 0.92 and 0.90. Our index also correlated with established indices, while null controls stayed near zero.
  Our index demonstrated hourly prognostic information that meaningfully separates patient outcomes and is consistent with clinical expectation, indicating potential as a decision support tool complementing clinical judgement.

</details>


### [95] [Mechanistic Reaction Prediction via Discrete Flow Matching on Graph-Structured Electron Occupation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27429)
*Nguyen Xuan-Vu,Octavian Susanu,Daniel Armstrong,Philippe Schwaller*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MAELLE是一种基于离散流匹配（discrete flow matching）的化学反应预测模型，它不同于传统方法在分子拓扑上进行启发式编辑或从头生成产物，而是将化学反应建模为电子占据向量（electron occupation vectors）上的连续时间马尔可夫链（CTMC），并通过最优传输（Optimal Transport）构造中间编辑轨迹，从而获得可解释的机理编辑步骤。在USPTO-480K基准上达到与领先模型相当的性能，并在结构复杂性和反应类型两种分布外（OOD）场景下表现更稳健，还能自然恢复符合已知化学的机理轨迹并预测副产物。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习化学反应建模方法存在根本局限：de novo生成产物缺乏反应机理可解释性，而基于分子拓扑的启发式图编辑过于依赖于化学家的先验规则，无法捕捉反应本质上是电子空间重排这一核心化学事实。

Method: 将反应物到产物的映射形式化为定义在键合位点、非键合位点和氢位点上的整数电子占据空间的连续时间马尔可夫链（CTMC）；通过将离散流匹配混合路径推广到离散电子重排，结合最优传输构造中间编辑轨迹序列，无需基础步骤标注即可获得机理可解释的编辑动作。

Result: 在USPTO-480K基准上取得与领先反应预测模型相当的竞争性性能；在结构复杂性和反应类型两类分布外设置中，现有方法性能下降而MAELLE保持稳健性能；模型能够恢复与已知化学一致的机理轨迹并预测副产物。

Conclusion: 将反应建模为电子占据空间的离散流匹配是可行且优越的范式，在保持预测精度的同时提供机理可解释性、分布外鲁棒性和副产物预测能力，为反应预测提供了超越拓扑编辑的全新视角。

Abstract: Chemical reactions are fundamentally transformations in electron space, yet most machine learning approaches model them either through \textit{de novo} generation of product molecules or through heuristic graph edits that operate directly on molecular topology.
  We introduce MAELLE (\textbf{M}ech\textbf{A}nistic \textbf{E}dit f\textbf{L}ow-matching on e\textbf{L}ectron r\textbf{E}arrangements), which instead models reactions as discrete flow matching over electron occupation vectors.
  Concretely, we formulate the reactant-to-product mapping as a Continuous-time Markov Chain (CTMC) over the graph-structured integer-valued electron occupation space defined on all bonding, non-bonding, and hydrogen sites.
  To construct the intermediate edit trajectories, we generalize the discrete flow matching mixture path to discrete electron rearrangements using Optimal Transport, yielding a sequence of mechanistically interpretable edit moves without requiring elementary step annotations.
  MAELLE achieves competitive performance on the USPTO-480K benchmark compared with leading reaction prediction models.
  Beyond in-distribution accuracy, we evaluate robustness across two out-of-distribution settings - structural complexity and reaction type - and find that MAELLE maintains strong performance where existing methods degrade.
  Finally, because the learned flow operates over the full electron redistribution, MAELLE naturally recovers mechanistic trajectories that align with known chemistry and can predict side products of a reaction.

</details>


### [96] [WikiSkill: Compiling Agent Experience into Persistent Knowledge for Skill Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27454)
*Liyan Tang,Cyrus Rashtchian,Chun-Sung Ferng,Andrew Tomkins,Da-Cheng Juan,Tu Vu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: WikiSkill提出一种将智能体技能进化与持久化知识库（wiki）共同演化的框架，通过分离原始执行经验、累积知识和可执行技能，实现经验的系统性积累与复用，在多个基准和模型上优于现有技能进化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的技能自动发现方法虽然能让智能体通过交互渐进式适应，但指导技能开发的经验洞察往往分散在优化历史中，难以被系统性地跨迭代复用，限制了技能进化的效率和质量。

Method: WikiSkill框架将原始执行经验、累积知识与可执行技能三者分离：持续将执行经验整合（consolidating）到wiki知识库中，后续的技能更新可以在该知识库基础上构建，实现技能与知识库的协同演化。

Result: 在多个基准和模型上，WikiSkill一致优于最先进的技能进化方法，且在大多数模型-基准组合中优于无技能基线。技能进化与模型规模互补：大型模型从进化技能中获益更多，而带技能的小型模型可以超越不带技能的更大模型。进化技能在模型及模型家族间可迁移，且其他模型进化的技能甚至可优于自进化技能。消融实验确认wiki中的持久知识积累对有效技能进化至关重要。

Conclusion: 系统性地积累和精炼智能体经验对于开发可复用、可迁移的技能至关重要，WikiSkill证明了持久化知识库在技能进化中的核心价值。

Abstract: Agent skills package specialized knowledge and workflows into reusable resources that extend AI agent capabilities. Recent work automatically discovers such skills from agent experience, which enables agents to progressively adapt through interaction. However, the insights that guide skill development typically remain scattered across optimization histories, limiting their systematic reuse across iterations. We introduce WikiSkill, a framework that co-evolves agent skills with a persistent knowledge base (wiki). At a high level, WikiSkill separates raw execution experience, accumulated knowledge, and executable skills, while continuously consolidating experience into the wiki, which subsequent skill updates can build on. Across diverse benchmarks and models, WikiSkill consistently outperforms state-of-the-art skill-evolution methods and improves over no-skill baselines in most model-benchmark settings. We find that skill evolution complements model scaling: larger models generally benefit more from evolved skills, while smaller models with skills can outperform substantially larger models without them. We also find that evolved skills transfer effectively across models and model families, and skills evolved by other models can outperform self-evolved skills. Finally, our ablation studies confirm that persistent knowledge accumulation in the wiki is critical for effective skill evolution. These results demonstrate the benefits of systematically accumulating and refining agent experience for developing reusable and transferable skills.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [97] [Assessing the Downstream Utility of Evidence-Aware Retrieval in RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26379)
*Utshab Kumar Ghosh,Debayan Mukhopadhyay,Shubham Chatterjee*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文研究了RAG中基于答案证据的检索评估信号在四种下游角色中的效用，发现该信号虽能改变检索排名，但其下游价值并不统一：不能可靠改善检索器训练、系统选择收益取决于生成器指令、无法稳健预测未见主题的答案质量，且人工验证显示过滤保留有用证据时不同评估器结论不一。因此，RAG评估方法应根据其具体支持的比较、决策和结论来评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG领域的检索评估日益趋向于以检索段落能否支持生成作为标准，而不仅仅看主题相关性。作者希望探究这种与下游证据需求更紧密对齐的评估信号，是否真的能使基于这些评估所做的决策更可靠、更有用。

Method: 作者在五个检索基准和端到端的TREC RAG 2025设置下，将答案支持信号应用于四个角色：(1)比较检索器，(2)指导检索训练和系统选择，(3)预测下游答案质量，(4)过滤提供给生成器的证据。并通过人类标注验证过滤效果。

Result: 答案支持信号确实改变了检索排名，但下游价值不统一：无法可靠改善检索器训练；系统选择中的收益取决于生成器如何被指示使用检索证据；基于该信号的检索分数不能稳健预测未见主题的答案质量；直接证据过滤干预中，人类确认过滤能保留有用证据，但不同答案评估器对答案是否改善给出不同结论。

Conclusion: 使检索评估更紧密反映生成所需证据，并不会自动使评估的每个下游用途更可靠。RAG评估方法应针对其具体支持的比较、决策和结论进行针对性评估，而非假设对齐信号在所有场景下都有统一价值。

Abstract: Retrieval evaluation for retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) is increasingly designed around whether retrieved passages contain evidence that can support generation, rather than topical relevance alone. We study whether this closer alignment with downstream evidence needs also makes retrieval evaluation more useful for the decisions built from it.
  Across five retrieval benchmarks and an end-to-end TREC RAG 2025 setting, we examine an answer-support signal in four roles: comparing retrievers, guiding retrieval training and system selection, predicting downstream answer quality, and filtering the evidence supplied to a generator. The signal changes retrieval rankings, but its downstream value is not uniform. It does not reliably improve retriever training; the benefit of using it for system selection depends on how the generator is instructed to use the retrieved evidence; and retrieval scores based on it do not robustly predict answer quality on unseen topics. In a direct evidence intervention, human annotators confirm that filtering preferentially preserves passages containing useful answer evidence, yet different answer evaluators reach different conclusions about whether the resulting answers improve.
  These results show that making retrieval evaluation more closely reflect the evidence needed for generation does not by itself make every downstream use of that evaluation more reliable. RAG evaluation methods should therefore be assessed with respect to the particular comparisons, decisions, and conclusions they are intended to support.

</details>


### [98] [Preference Flow Matching with Spectral Factorization for Micro-video Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26579)
*Xinxin Dong,Haokai Ma,Fei Hu,YuZe Zheng,Bin Wu,Yonghui Yang,Xiaodong Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: PrismRec 提出了一种基于谱分解的偏好流匹配框架用于微视频推荐，通过将视频帧序列分解为互补的静态语义因子与动态因子，并以用户敏感度校准的上下文作为结构化条件引导流匹配轨迹生成目标表示，在四个数据集上超越SOTA基线最多22.65%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有微视频推荐方法存在两个核心问题：一是将帧序列压缩为单一整体表示，混淆了稳定的视觉语义与演变的动态特征，而这两者共同构成用户偏好；二是基于扩散或流匹配的推荐器仅以粗糙的行为上下文为条件，未将内部时间结构纳入偏好形成过程，导致视频内容沦为辅助信息而非内在驱动。

Method: PrismRec包含两个核心模块：(1) 谱语义因子分解（SSF），类比棱镜分光原理，通过先验引导的可学习频率掩码在时域频率域将帧级表示分解为互补的静态语义因子和动态因子；(2) 上下文校准偏好匹配（CPM），以每个用户对两类因子的特定敏感度进行加权，将校准后的上下文作为结构化条件注入匹配轨迹，引导生成过程朝向目标表示。

Result: 在来自两个平台的四个数据集上，PrismRec相比SOTA基线最高提升22.65%，且在对比方法中拥有最低的推理成本和峰值内存占用。

Conclusion: 通过将视频内容解耦为静态与动态语义因子并以用户感知校准的方式融入流匹配生成过程，PrismRec使视频内容成为偏好形成的内在驱动力，验证了频率域分解与结构化条件注入在微视频推荐中的有效性。

Abstract: Micro-video recommendation aims to infer user preferences from historical interactions and multimodal video content, thereby identifying the next video of interest. However, prevailing methods compress frame sequences into a single holistic representation, entangling the stable visual semantics and the evolving dynamics that jointly shape user preferences. Meanwhile, diffusion- and flow matching-based recommenders condition their generation process solely on coarse behavioral context, leaving its internal temporal structure outside preference formation. We therefore propose PrismRec, a Preference Flow Matching framework with Spectral Factorization for Micro-video Recommendation. Analogous to a prism that disperses white light into its constituent spectrum, PrismRec devises Spectral Semantic Factorization (SSF) to derive complementary static semantic and dynamic factors from frame-level representations via a prior-guided learnable frequency mask in the temporal frequency domain. Then, it proposes Context-Calibrated Preference Matching (CPM) to weigh them with each user's specific sensitivity and inject the calibrated context as a structured condition to steer the matching trajectory toward the target representation, making video content as an intrinsic driver of preference formation rather than auxiliary side information. Experiments on four datasets from two platforms show that PrismRec surpasses the SOTA baseline by up to 22.65%, with the lowest inference cost and peak memory among the compared methods.

</details>


### [99] [hoBIT: A Profile-Aware Retrieval-Augmented Chatbot for University Academic Advising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26604)
*Yoonseo Kim,Seongmin Lee,Joongheon Kim,SeongKu Kang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: proFILL将大学学术咨询聊天机器人hoBIT从基于规则的系统升级为个人资料感知的检索增强生成(RAG)系统，逐步获取每个查询所需的用户档案属性（如院系、入学批次、学位课程），并据此调节检索过程，从而为不同背景的学生提供准确适配的答案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大学学术咨询场景中，相同的问题可能因学生的院系、入学批次和学位项目不同而需要不同的答案。传统的"档案盲"检索器会检索出看似合理但不适用的证据，导致回答不准确。现有方案需要用户一次性提供完整档案，体验不佳，因此需要一种能够按需渐进式获取用户属性并进行档案感知检索的方法。

Method: proFILL将hoBIT（现有基于规则的咨询聊天机器人）转变为档案感知的RAG系统。其核心策略是：(1) 不要求用户一开始就提供完整档案，而是根据查询意图和初始检索到的证据，渐进式地仅获取每个查询所需的必要档案属性；(2) 使用获取到的档案属性对检索过程进行条件化，在档案感知索引上进行检索。

Result: 广泛的实验和人工偏好研究表明，proFILL在多种RAG基线上表现出更优的性能，得到目标用户的偏好选择，并且在使用开源权重模型时仍然保持有效，可支持成本效益高的本地部署。

Conclusion: proFILL验证了渐进式档案获取与档案感知检索相结合的有效性，能在不过度打扰用户的前提下显著提升学术咨询系统的回答准确性，同时具备实用性（支持开源模型本地部署）的优势。

Abstract: In university academic advising, identical questions can require different answers depending on a student's department, admission cohort, and degree program, causing profile-blind retrievers to surface plausible but inapplicable evidence. We present proFILL, a method for transforming hoBIT, our college's current rule-based advising chatbot, into a profile-aware retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) system. Rather than requiring a complete user profile upfront, proFILL progressively acquires only the profile attributes needed for each query, guided by both the query intent and the initially retrieved evidence, and uses them to condition retrieval over a profile-aware index. Extensive experiments and a human preference study show that proFILL outperforms diverse RAG baselines, is preferred by target users, and remains effective with open-weight models for cost-effective on-premise deployment.

</details>


### [100] [When Does Supervised Fine-Tuning Reduce Instruction Sensitivity?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26661)
*Jaekeol Choi*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文研究了监督微调（SFT）如何改变大语言模型对指令表述变化的敏感性。通过对Qwen3（1.7B/4B/8B）、Mistral-7B和Gemma-2-9B的受控实验发现：SFT并不统一降低指令敏感性，其效果取决于模型规模、跨家族差异以及评估协议（自由生成vs似然强制选择）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型对同一任务的不同指令表述会表现出显著的性能波动，但传统的任务特定监督微调（SFT）如何改变这种指令敏感性尚不清楚。理解这一关系对于可靠地部署和评估微调后的模型至关重要。

Method: 在固定模型检查点上使用多个改写后的指令进行评估，将指令敏感性定义为跨指令任务性能的标准差。对Qwen3在1.7B、4B和8B规模上进行受控规模分析（MS MARCO数据集），并用Mistral-7B和Gemma-2-9B进行跨家族检查。采用query-level bootstrap分析检验统计显著性，并在ESCI-English数据集上对比自由生成与基于似然的强制选择两种评估协议。

Result: SFT前，指令敏感性随Qwen3模型规模增大而急剧下降。在1.7B和4B规模，SFT将敏感性降低了约54-71%。在8B规模，个体敏感性变化统计上与零不可区分，但训练指令之间的配对对比在bootstrap分析下统计可靠且三种随机种子方向一致。Gemma-2-9B显示出与Qwen3-8B相同的训练指令对比方向，而Mistral-7B则不然。此外，自由生成与似然强制选择评估可能产生定性不同的鲁棒性结论。

Conclusion: SFT并非统一地降低指令敏感性：其鲁棒性效果取决于适应设置，而测得的敏感性还取决于预测和评分协议。这意味着在评估微调模型的指令鲁棒性时，需要同时考虑模型规模、跨模型族差异和评估协议的影响。

Abstract: Large language models can exhibit substantial performance variation across alternative formulations of the same task instruction, yet it remains unclear how conventional task-specific supervised fine-tuning (SFT) changes this instruction sensitivity. We study this question by evaluating fixed model checkpoints under multiple paraphrased instructions and defining instruction sensitivity as the standard deviation of task performance across them. We conduct a controlled scale analysis with Qwen3 models at 1.7B, 4B, and 8B on MS MARCO, together with targeted cross-family checks using Mistral-7B and Gemma-2-9B. Before SFT, instruction sensitivity decreases sharply with Qwen3 model scale. At 1.7B and 4B, SFT consistently reduces sensitivity across training instructions, with reductions of approximately 54--71%. At 8B, individual sensitivity changes are not statistically distinguishable from zero, but paired contrasts between training instructions are statistically reliable under query-level bootstrap analysis and have consistent directions across all three random seeds. Gemma-2-9B shows the same directional training-instruction contrast as Qwen3-8B, whereas Mistral-7B does not, suggesting that the strength of this effect also varies across models. Experiments on ESCI-English further show that free-generation and likelihood-based forced-choice evaluation can yield qualitatively different robustness conclusions even when valid-label generation is nearly perfect and average task performance is similar. Overall, SFT does not uniformly reduce instruction sensitivity: its robustness effect depends on the adaptation setting, while measured sensitivity can additionally depend on the prediction and scoring protocol.

</details>


### [101] [BLANC: Discovering Patent White Space via Changes in Normalized Pointwise Mutual Information Between Multi-View Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26685)
*Shuichi Miyazawa,Kensuke Fujii*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出BLANC管道，通过三阶段流程（多视角神经主题建模、NPMI量化、条件检测）识别专利空白景观区域，并通过人工耗尽已知技术组合的方式验证其恢复能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专利空白区域（white space）的识别对战略研发规划至关重要，但现有方法依赖人工专利绘图或使用单视角聚类且缺乏定量缺口检测，无法系统、自动化地识别"全局已确立但局部未探索"的技术组合。

Method: BLANC由三阶段构成：(1) 沿应用/用途、新颖性、创造性三个语义维度进行多视角神经主题建模；(2) 使用NPMI量化跨维度聚类间的关联；(3) 通过条件检测（按用户指定关键词过滤语料库）识别NPMI下降的组合，并以新指标ΔNPMI表征"全局已确立、局部未探索"的空白区域。评估采用两个公开USPTO语料库人工耗尽已知技术组合并测试恢复率。

Result: 在删除目标组合四分之三文档后，BLANC在ML/AI和玻璃语料库上分别恢复了34.1%和27.3%的耗尽组合；191次诱饵试验中目标组合从未被恢复。将三视角合并为单一视角时一无所获，而先前的共现指标在随机删除下也会错误标记目标，缺乏特异性。在专有浮法玻璃/玻璃陶瓷案例中，关键词"氟"揭示了专家独立识别的氟表面处理×翘曲抑制候选组合（ΔNPMI最高0.48）。

Conclusion: BLANC通过多视角主题建模与NPMI条件检测，能够特异性且有效地识别专利空白区域，显著优于单视角聚类和传统共现指标，为专利景观空白分析提供了可量化的自动化解决方案。

Abstract: Identifying white space --- the unexplored but potentially valuable regions of a patent landscape --- is essential for strategic R&D planning, yet existing methods rely on manual patent mapping or apply single-view clustering without quantitative gap detection. We propose BLANC (Blank Landscape Analysis through NPMI Conditioning), a three-phase pipeline combining (1) multi-view neural topic modeling along three semantic dimensions (application/use, novelty, inventive step); (2) Normalized Pointwise Mutual Information (NPMI) to quantify cross-dimensional cluster association; and (3) conditional detection that flags combinations whose NPMI drops when the corpus is filtered by a user-specified keyword. The drop is captured by a new metric, $Δ$NPMI, which identifies combinations "established globally, unexplored locally." Because white space has no ground truth, we evaluate BLANC on two public USPTO corpora --- machine learning/AI (5,417 patents, CPC G06N) and glass compositions (1,982 patents, CPC C03C) --- by artificially depleting known technology combinations and testing recovery. When three-quarters of a target pair's documents are removed, BLANC recovers 34.1% (ML/AI) and 27.3% (glass) of the depleted combinations, whereas size-matched removals not aimed at them (random documents, or those of a different established combination) essentially never do: the target is never recovered in 191 decoy trials. Collapsing the three semantic views into one recovers nothing, while prior co-occurrence measures also flag the target under random removal, offering no specificity. In a proprietary case (302 float glass / glass-ceramics patents), the keyword "fluorine" reveals a fluorine surface treatment $\times$ warpage suppression candidate ($Δ$NPMI up to 0.48) that experts had independently identified.

</details>


### [102] [Beyond a Single Story: Meta-Reviewing Sparse and Incomplete User-generated Contents for Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26728)
*Hongren Wang,Tianjun Wei,Yingpeng Du,Jie Zhang,Yin-Leng Theng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出MOSAIC框架，通过从邻居用户评论中聚合属性-情感证据构建元评论（meta-review），利用MMoE架构联合优化评分预测和元评论属性-情感预测，以缓解用户生成内容（UGC）中缺失评论和不完整评论带来的数据稀疏问题，在推荐准确率和解释质量上均优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推荐系统中的数据稀疏问题长期以来是挑战，而依赖文本评论等用户生成内容（UGC）的方法面临更严重的稀疏问题。UGC存在两类特有缺陷：(1) 缺失评论——交互缺少评论；(2) 不完整评论——已有评论仅覆盖部分相关属性。现有方法往往忽视这些UGC特有问题，导致推荐精度下降。

Method: 借鉴学术同行评审中的元评论（meta-review）概念：为每个目标用户构建元评论，通过聚合邻居用户评论中的属性-情感证据实现。采用多门控混合专家（MMoE）架构联合优化评分预测与元评论的属性-情感预测任务，并通过注意力模块将聚合的元评论信号个性化适配到每个目标用户，同时产出精细化的评分预测和属性级解释。

Result: 在四个真实世界数据集上的实验表明，MOSAIC在推荐准确率和解释质量两方面均一致优于最先进的基线方法，有效缓解了UGC稀疏和不完整问题，且对交互历史有限的用户也能带来一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: MOSAIC通过元评论聚合和MMoE多任务学习，成功缓解了UGC稀疏性与不完整性对推荐系统的负面影响，同时提供了属性级可解释性，为基于评论的推荐系统在稀疏场景下的性能提升提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Data sparsity remains a long-standing challenge in recommender systems, and it becomes more severe for methods relying on user-generated content (UGC) such as textual reviews, which capture fine-grained preferences but require more user efforts to produce. As a result, UGC exhibits (1) missing reviews, where interactions lack any review, and (2) incomplete reviews, where available reviews cover only a subset of relevant attributes. Existing approaches often overlook these UGC-specific issues, leading to degraded accuracy. Motivated by meta-review in academic peer review, we propose MOSAIC (Meta-review On Sparse And Incomplete user-generated Content), which constructs a meta-review for each target user by aggregating attribute-sentiment evidence from neighbor users' reviews. A multi-gate mixture-of-experts (MMoE) architecture jointly optimizes rating prediction and meta-review attribute-sentiment prediction, while an attention module personalizes the aggregated meta-review signals to each target user, yielding both refined rating predictions and attribute-level explanations. Experiments on four real-world datasets demonstrate that MOSAIC consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in both recommendation accuracy and explanation quality, mitigating UGC sparsity and incompleteness while delivering consistent gains for users with limited interaction history.

</details>


### [103] [STREAM: An Objective-Driven and Uncertainty-Aware Framework for Industrial Energy Data Acquisition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26754)
*Zhipeng Ma,Bo Nørregaard Jørgensen,Zheng Grace Ma*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: STREAM是一个目标驱动且考虑不确定性的工业能源数据采集框架，涵盖从目标定义到数据库迁移的六个阶段，通过两个工业批量生产案例验证了数据可访问性不等于分析适用性，并支持透明决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统工业能源数据采集工作流通常只关注连接性和存储，而未验证可访问信号是否满足能源绩效评估的需求，导致数据虽可访问但分析上不适用。需要一个客观驱动、能系统评估数据适用性的框架来填补这一缺口。

Method: STREAM框架包含六个阶段：目标规范、技术要求、资源映射、源提取、归档元数据和数据库迁移。新增了阶段级工件、最小证据门槛、源适用性规则、元数据模板、不确定性规则和案例特定的可追溯性矩阵。跨六个阶段评估测量、时间、上下文和处理四类不确定性。

Result: 通过两个工业批量流程案例（铸造厂感应炉熔炼和奶酪粉干燥，基于SCADA和生产订单数据）验证，结果表明数据可访问性不等于分析适用性，STREAM能支持关于数据即时使用、分析限制和优先基础设施改进的透明决策。

Conclusion: STREAM将目标到数据的可追溯性作为端到端产出，为工业能源管理中的数据采集提供了系统化、可审计的流程，弥补了数据连接存储与分析适用性之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Industrial energy management requires datasets that connect energy use with equipment states, production batches, material flows, and process conditions. However, conventional acquisition workflows commonly emphasize connectivity and storage without verifying whether accessible signals satisfy the requirements of a defined energy-performance assessment. This paper presents STREAM, an objective-driven and uncertainty-aware framework comprising Specification of Objectives, Technical Requirements, Resource Mapping, Extraction from Sources, Archival Metadata, and Migration to Database. STREAM is the central workflow: objective-to-data traceability is its end-to-end output, while measurement, temporal, contextual, and processing uncertainty are assessed across all six stages. Compared with the original conceptual STREAM sequence, this paper adds stage-level artifacts, minimum-evidence gates, source-suitability rules, a metadata template, an uncertainty rubric, and case-specific traceability matrices. The framework is validated through two industrial batch-process cases: induction-furnace melting in a foundry and cheese-powder drying using SCADA and production-order data. The results demonstrate that data accessibility is not equivalent to analytical suitability and show how STREAM supports transparent decisions about immediate data use, analytical restrictions, and prioritized infrastructure improvements.

</details>


### [104] [When Memory Takes Gradients: Collaborative Vector Memory for Agentic Recommender Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26895)
*Hanchong Chen,Xing Tang,Lingjie Li,Xiongfeng Shan,Xiuqiang He*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出CoVeMem（协作向量记忆）框架，将智能体推荐系统中大语言模型的持久记忆从文本形式改为向量形式，利用冻结的LightGCN用户和物品状态构建记忆库，通过对比对齐和列表式协同训练使LLM读取并利用这些向量状态进行排序，在四个指令基推荐基准上优于最强文本记忆智能体，且无需额外LLM调用来维护记忆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体推荐系统将用户记忆以文本形式存储，由LLM逐次重写维护，这带来两个根本限制：一是记忆只能一次一次重写，无法高效利用完整交互历史；二是协同过滤证据（对整个目录的相似度评分）无法在翻译成句子的过程中得到保留。因此需要一种能够保持协作信息、且支持梯度训练的记忆表征方式。

Method: CoVeMem将智能体记忆的协作核心向量化：用冻结的LightGCN用户态和物品态构建记忆库；在每个决策时，候选集本身检索最相关的历史状态作为软标记（soft tokens）注入LLM上下文，并辅以轻量文本画像；通过对比对齐到物品语义锚点，再配合掩码候选的列表式（listwise）协同训练，教会模型读取这些状态并进行排序；最后用逐点（pointwise）是/否读出为每个候选打分。

Result: 在四个指令基推荐基准上，CoVeMem在20个指标单元中的19个上达到或超过最强的协作文本记忆智能体；且记忆维护无需任何额外LLM调用（仅共享静态画像），而文本记忆智能体需要每次交互调用LLM。

Conclusion: CoVeMem表明将记忆向量化不仅消除了文本记忆的维护成本和解码瓶颈，还使完整交互历史可参与梯度训练，即"记忆现在可以接受梯度"——既优化了智能体记住什么，也优化了它如何读取所记住的内容，为推荐智能体的记忆设计提供了新方向。

Abstract: Agentic recommender systems ground each decision of a large language model (LLM) in a persistent memory of the user, and in existing agents that memory is text: a narrative written and maintained by further LLM calls. Text limits this memory in two ways. It is updated one rewrite at a time, so exploiting the full interaction history is prohibitively expensive; and collaborative evidence, graded similarity over an entire catalog, does not survive translation into sentences. We propose CoVeMem (Collaborative Vector Memory), which vectorizes the collaborative core of the agent's memory. Frozen LightGCN user and item states form the memory bank; at each decision, the candidate set itself retrieves the most relevant historical states, which enter the LLM's context as soft tokens alongside a light textual profile. Contrastive alignment to item-semantic anchors, followed by listwise co-training with masked candidates, teaches the model to read these states and to rank through them; a pointwise yes/no readout scores each candidate. Across four instruction-grounded recommendation benchmarks, CoVeMem matches or exceeds the strongest collaborative text-memory agent on 19 of 20 metric cells while requiring zero additional LLM calls for memory maintenance beyond the shared static profile, against per-interaction calls for text memory. The memory now takes gradients: the full interaction history, out of reach for text, becomes available as training data for what the agent remembers and for how it reads what it remembers.

</details>


### [105] [Topology-Masked Unified Backbone for Joint Feature Interaction and Multi-Domain Sequence Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27005)
*Zhihao Zhu,Dezheng Han,Jikang Xia,Shuaishuai Guo*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: MaskRec是一种面向工业CVR预测的拓扑掩码统一Token交互架构，将异构特征、多域行为序列和上下文信号统一为Token表示，通过结构化注意力掩码（TopoMask）在同一拓扑约束空间中同时完成特征交互与多域序列建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的工业排序模型通常将特征交互和多域用户行为序列建模分为两个独立模块处理，而最近的统一架构虽然尝试将其整合到单一框架中，但往往依赖模块间的协调，未能将所有信息源充分组织在同一交互空间中，导致信息融合不充分。

Method: MaskRec首先将异构特征、多域行为序列和上下文信号统一转化为Token表示，引入可学习的全局记忆Token和域级记忆Token作为信息聚合节点；在此基础上设计结构化注意力掩码TopoMask，根据信息源的结构差异和建模需求选择性地启用或阻断注意力连接；同时提出双路径交互式查询生成模块，在统一骨干网络之前注入候选条件化的用户-物品交互信号。

Result: 在腾讯广告算法大赛数据集上的实验表明，MaskRec相比官方基线取得了稳定的性能提升，验证了所提出的统一框架在工业CVR预测场景中的有效性。

Conclusion: MaskRec通过在统一的Token空间中施加拓扑约束的注意力机制，成功将异构特征交互与多域序列建模融合到同一交互过程，为工业大規模CVR预测提供了一种有效的统一建模方案。

Abstract: Large-scale post-click conversion rate (CVR) prediction requires jointly modeling heterogeneous feature interactions and dependencies over multi-domain user behavior sequences. Existing industrial ranking models usually handle these two aspects with separate modules. Recent unified architectures attempt to incorporate them into a single framework, but such unification often relies on coordination between modules and does not fully organize all information sources within the same interaction space. To address this problem, we propose MaskRec, a topology-masked unified token interaction architecture for feature interaction and multi-domain sequence modeling. MaskRec transforms heterogeneous features, multi-domain behavior sequences, and contextual signals into unified token representations, and further introduces learnable global memory tokens and domain-level memory tokens as information aggregation nodes. Based on this unified token space, MaskRec designs a structured attention mask, TopoMask, which selectively enables or blocks attention connections according to the structural differences and modeling requirements of different information sources. In this way, heterogeneous feature interaction and multi-domain sequence modeling are performed within the same topology-constrained attention process. In addition, MaskRec incorporates a dual-path interactive query generation module to inject candidate-conditioned user--item interaction signals before the unified backbone. Experiments on the Tencent Advertising Algorithm Competition dataset show that MaskRec achieves stable improvements over the official baseline, validating the effectiveness of the proposed unified framework for industrial CVR prediction.

</details>


### [106] [Conversational Recommendation over Live E-Commerce Catalogues with Self-Refreshing Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27006)
*Ante Kapetanovic,Tomislav Duricic,Dionizije Fa,Andro Mercep,Emanuel Lacic*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于LLM的多轮对话购物助手，核心是自刷新检索器，通过哈希检测商品变更并对增量数据进行处理，从而支持在实时变化的电商目录上进行推荐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于大语言模型的对话式推荐系统通常在静态、预索引的商品集合上评估，而实际电商目录会持续变化（商品增删、改价、补货等），传统静态索引无法适应这种动态场景。

Method: 系统包含两个核心模块：(1) 自刷新检索器，摄取商家商品feed并进行富化，同步至向量索引，利用每个商品的哈希标识识别新增/变更/删除/未变的商品，仅处理增量数据而无需重建整个目录；(2) 基于控制器的对话层，仅使用LLM进行意图分类和偏好提取，而检索、重排和多样性选择由专用函数完成。

Result: 在WhatsApp上部署了一个实时购物助手作为演示，目录变更在下一次成功同步后即可反映到推荐结果中，并提供公开可访问的代码仓库、文档和录制演示。

Conclusion: 通过增量同步机制和将LLM仅用于对话理解的架构设计，实现了能够在实时变化的电商目录上高效运行的对话式购物推荐系统，兼顾了效率和实时性。

Abstract: Conversational recommender systems based on large language models (LLMs) are usually evaluated on static, pre-indexed item collections, yet e-commerce catalogues change continuously as products are added or removed, repriced, and restocked. We present a merchant-agnostic, multi-turn conversational shopping assistant that operates over such live catalogues. Its central component is a self-refreshing retriever that ingests a merchant product feed, enriches the records, and synchronizes them into a vector index. On each run, per-item hashes identify which products are new, changed, deleted, or unchanged, so only the delta is processed rather than rebuilding the whole catalogue. A controller-based dialogue layer consumes this index, using an LLM only for intent classification and preference elicitation while retrieval, reranking, and diversity selection run as dedicated functions. Our demonstration is a WhatsApp shopping assistant in which catalogue changes reach the recommendations after the next successful sync. A live chatbot, documentation, and a recorded walkthrough are available at https://github.com/infobip/infobip-agentic-crs.

</details>


### [107] [ProRetrieval: Learning to Orchestrate Hybrid Search via Executable Program Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27017)
*Chengsong You,Zhen Sun,Yunhai Hu,Junwei Zhou,Xiaoyu Cao,Binyu Li,Ziyan Zhao,Weiyao Wang,Liren Lu,Zhijie Ye,Yumo Cao,Yitao Long,Yiwei Xu,Qiyi Jiang,Xuanyi Fu,Yufan Chen,Yilun Li,Rongkang Xiong,Yiran Zou,Nan Du*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: ProRetrieval将语言模型重新定义为检索编排器，面对自然语言查询时生成一个混合DSL的可执行程序，该程序将SQL运算符（用于结构化字段）与向量检索原语（用于文本和图像）交织在一起，由SQL本身提供逻辑代数来融合异构候选集。作者用GRPO和DAPO训练Qwen3-4B，并在两个新基准上超越了GPT-5.5和Claude Opus 4.7等强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界的检索场景常常需要将结构化约束与跨文本/图像的语义意图通过任意布尔逻辑组合起来。现有方法（如倒数排名融合或自查询检索器）仅支持固定形式的组合，而最近的强化学习检索器仅将语言模型训练为单一后端的查询生成器，异构检索路径的编排不在其动作空间内。

Method: ProRetrieval将语言模型视为检索编排器：给定自然语言查询，它合成一个混合DSL中的可执行程序，该DSL将结构化字段上的SQL运算符与文本和图像上的向量检索原语交织；SQL本身提供逻辑代数来融合异构候选集。使用GRPO和DAPO在分层四项奖励下训练Qwen3-4B，并在从Amazon产品和Enron邮件构建的两个新基准上评估。

Result: 4B模型在电子商务场景Hit@1达0.81（对比GPT-5.5的0.69），在邮件场景达0.91（对比0.86），超过GPT-5.5和Claude Opus 4.7以及全面的一系列检索、LLM增强、结构化查询和图基基线。

Conclusion: 将语言模型训练为混合检索编排器可以显著超越更大规模通用模型，在异构检索路径的编排上展示了代码和资源的可用性，表明该范式是应对复杂组合检索的有效方案。

Abstract: Real-world retrieval often composes structured constraints with semantic intents over text and images through arbitrary Boolean logic. Existing hybrid pipelines such as reciprocal rank fusion or self-querying retrievers admit only a fixed form of composition, while recent reinforcement-learning retrievers train the language model as a query generator for a single backend, leaving the orchestration of heterogeneous retrieval paths outside its action space. We propose ProRetrieval, which recasts the language model as a retrieval orchestrator: given a natural-language query, it synthesizes an executable program in a hybrid DSL interleaving SQL operators over structured fields with vector-retrieval primitives over text and images, with SQL itself providing the logical algebra that fuses heterogeneous candidate sets. We train Qwen3-4B with GRPO and DAPO under a hierarchical four-term reward, and evaluate on two new benchmarks built from Amazon products and Enron email. Our 4B model surpasses GPT-5.5 (Hit@1 0.81 vs. 0.69 on e-commerce; 0.91 vs. 0.86 on email) and Claude Opus 4.7 and a comprehensive suite of retrieval, LLM-augmented, structured-query, and graph-based baselines. Code: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/ProRetrieval/; data: https://huggingface.co/datasets/anonymous-7219/ProRetrieval.

</details>


### [108] [Astar: Learning to Propose Evolution Directions for Self-Evolving Industrial AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27287)
*Jinxin Hu,Hao Deng,Haibo Xing,Lingyu Mu,Muyu Zou,Weiqin Yang,Sirui Chen,Bohao Wang,Zhezheng Hao,Hao Zhang,Zulong Chen,Shizhun Wang,Yu Zhang,Xiaoyi Zeng,Jiawei Chen*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出Astar，一个基于训练的方法，从工业系统的大量迭代历史中学习专门的演化方向生成模型，旨在自动化AI系统迭代循环中最关键的瓶颈——提出有效的演化方向，并在阿里巴巴Lazada广告系统中成功部署，实现了全自动迭代。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代AI系统通过持续迭代演进（提出方向→实现代码→训练→评估），虽然后三个阶段已日益自动化，但起点——提出有效的演化方向——仍是关键瓶颈，高度依赖资深专家。研究发现通用LLM（甚至GPT-5.5）只能提供泛泛且不一致的建议，因为相关专业知识是通过经验积累而非显式编码的，难以直接注入模型。

Method: Astar从工业系统丰富的迭代历史中学习专门的演化引导模型，解决四个挑战：1）数据侧：设计pipeline将嘈杂的历史提交转换为大规模干净的演化语料库，通过成对样本扩展和噪声过滤实现；2）模型侧：通过中期训练（mid-training）、SFT和RL三个阶段训练模型，用分层提示引导演化方向生成，并使用RL中的奖励模型作为快速替代评估器。

Result: Astar在阿里巴巴Lazada广告系统中部署用于演化方向提案。Astar-8B在真实执行评估中单次提案成功率达0.6786，远超人类专家（0.3229）和最强通用LLM（0.3071）。Astar实现了闭环全自动迭代：两周内连续引导20次迭代，离线Hitrate@200提升23.6%，在线A/B测试中GMV相对提升4.86%，广告收入提升1.82%。

Conclusion: Astar证明了AI可以接管AI系统演化方向提案这一此前高度依赖专家的角色，通过从历史迭代数据中学习而非依赖显式编码的知识，成功实现了AI系统的全自动闭环迭代，在实际工业部署中显著超越人类专家和通用大模型。

Abstract: Modern AI systems advance through continuous iteration: a loop of proposing evolution directions, implementing code, training, and evaluation. While the latter three stages are increasingly automated, the starting point --- proposing effective evolution directions --- remains a critical bottleneck that still relies heavily on senior experts. In this work, we explore whether AI can take over this role. We find that general-purpose LLMs, even the advanced GPT-5.5, offer only generic and misaligned suggestions: the required expertise is accumulated through experience rather than explicitly codified, and thus hard to inject directly.
  To this end, we propose Astar, a training-based approach that learns a specialized evolution-guiding model from the abundant iteration histories of industrial systems. Realizing this idea, however, raises four challenges: sparse supervision, noisy data, a vast direction space, and prohibitively expensive verification. We address them along two fronts. On the data side, we design a pipeline that turns noisy historical commits into a large, clean evolutionary corpus via pairwise sample expansion and noise filtering. On the model side, we train the model through mid-training, SFT, and RL, guiding evolution direction generation with hierarchical hints and using the reward model in RL as a fast surrogate evaluator.
  Astar has been deployed in Alibaba's Lazada advertising system for evolution direction proposal. Astar-8B achieves a single-proposal success rate of 0.6786 in real-execution evaluation, far exceeding human experts (0.3229) and the strongest general-purpose LLM (0.3071). More importantly, Astar closes the loop and enables fully automatic iteration: it guided 20 consecutive iterations over two weeks, improving offline Hitrate@200 by 23.6%, while an online A/B test yielded relative lifts of 4.86% in GMV and 1.82% in advertising revenue.

</details>


### [109] [Stageboost: Recommending Signals Based on Counterfactual Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27366)
*Darpan Singhal,Matan Mandelbrod,Tal Franji,Manasa Kolla,Vipul Gaba,Yuri Brovman*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个基于两阶段XGBoost的模型，用于优化eBay商品详情页（View-Item）上信号（signals）的展示，实验显示整体GMB提升0.08%，零配件类目GMB提升0.58%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: eBay商品详情页上的信号（如文本或视觉片段）旨在为用户提供额外的上下文信息，促进智能购买决策并激励用户参与。如何最优地选择和展示这些信号以最大化业务指标是关键问题。

Method: 提出一个两阶段（2-stage）基于XGBoost的模型。第一阶段用于信号的初筛或排序，第二阶段用于精排或最优信号组合的选择，从而在有限的展示空间中智能填充VI页面信号。

Result: 在线实验中，模型带来了0.08%的整体GMB（总购买毛额）提升，以及0.58%的零配件与附件类目GMB提升，主要归因于高均价商品转化率的提高。

Conclusion: 两阶段XGBoost方法能有效优化商品详情页信号的展示，尤其对高均价商品的购买转化有显著正向影响，验证了该方法的业务价值。

Abstract: Signals are short textual or visual snippets displayed on the eBay View-Item (VI) page, providing additional, contextual information for users about the viewed item. The aim of displaying these signals is to facilitate intelligent purchase and to incentivize engagement. In this paper, we present a 2 stage xgboost based model that optimally populates the VI page with signals. This approach has shown a 0.08% lift in overall GMB (Gross Merchandise Bought) and 0.58% increase in Parts and Accessories GMB, primarily due to increase in conversion of high average price items in online experimentation.

</details>


### [110] [Scaling Graph Neural Networks for Friend Recommendation: Multi-Hash User Embeddings and Temporal Neighbor Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27413)
*Maksim Utushkin,Andrei Ovsiannikov,Alexander D'yakonov*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个面向生产级社交图谱（1.94亿用户，280亿条边）的可扩展端到端GNN排序系统，核心贡献是两种关键设计选择：多哈希ID嵌入将嵌入表大小减少98%以上且不损失排序质量，以及时间戳排序的CSR存储配合二分查找将每个节点的时序邻居采样成本从O(deg(v)+k)降低到O(log(deg(v))+k)。在线A/B测试显示好友添加量提升16%，唯一好友添加用户数提升11.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 好友推荐本质上是图结构问题，多跳社交上下文比用户属性更重要，但在数亿用户、数百亿边的生产规模社交图上部署消息传递GNN面临大量建模和系统挑战。现有工业级GNN系统要么忽略可训练ID嵌入，要么接受超过200GB的完整嵌入表；时序邻居采样虽理论上成熟，但现有实现需扫描完整邻接表，对拥有数万好友的用户不可行。

Method: 系统采用两个关键设计：(1) 多哈希ID嵌入作为主要节点表示，将ID嵌入表从超过200GB压缩98%以上，同时保持排序质量；(2) 实现时间戳排序的CSR存储配合二分查找，将时序邻居采样的每节点成本从O(deg(v)+k)降至O(log(deg(v))+k)。两个组件结合实现分布式训练和推理，并发布框架。

Result: 在1.94亿用户、280亿边的图谱上进行离线消融实验，分别隔离每个设计选择的贡献。在线A/B测试中，与强生产基线相比，系统将来自推荐的好友添加量提升16%，唯一好友添加者数量提升11.5%。

Conclusion: 多哈希ID嵌入与时序邻居采样的结合在生产规模社交图上不仅可扩展，还能带来可衡量的业务指标提升，证明这两个设计选择是工业级GNN推荐系统的关键组件，并已开源框架。

Abstract: Friend recommendation is inherently graph-structured: the relevance of a potential connection depends on multi-hop social context rather than user attributes alone. However, deploying message-passing GNNs on a production-scale social graph with hundreds of millions of users and tens of billions of edges requires addressing numerous modeling and systems challenges. We present a scalable end-to-end GNN ranking system for production social graphs, focusing on two design choices that are critical in this setting: multi-hash ID embeddings and temporal neighbor sampling. Multi-hash embeddings are common for high-cardinality features, but industrial GNN systems typically either ignore trainable IDs or accept full embedding tables, exceeding 200 GB for our graph. We integrate multi-hash as the primary node representation, reducing the ID-embedding table size by more than 98 percent while preserving ranking quality. Temporal neighbor sampling is well understood in principle, but existing implementations scan full adjacency lists, which is a non-starter for users with tens of thousands of friends. We implement timestamp-sorted CSR storage with binary search, reducing the per-node temporal sampling cost from $O(deg(v) + k)$ to $O(\log(deg(v)) + k)$. Beyond these components, we show that this combination scales and yields measurable production impact. On a graph with 194M users and 28B edges, offline ablations isolate each design choice's contribution. In an online A/B test, our system increases friend additions from recommendations by 16 percent and unique friend adders by 11.5 percent over a strong production baseline. We release our framework for distributed training and inference on large temporal graphs.

</details>


### [111] [misi: a Metric Inverted Sample Index](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27422)
*Edgar Chavez*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出misi，一种面向一般度量空间的近似最近邻搜索倒排索引。该索引以数据库的随机样本（规模与n成比例）作为词汇表，每个对象由其最近的k_b个样本点表示，查询通过idf加权的共享邻居投票后对C个候选进行精确验证完成。该构造将NAPP索引从常数个枢轴推广为线性规模词汇表，将倒排列表长度保持为常数期望值ρ=k_b/α，使索引成为"组合器"：任何在αn个点上达到高召回率的索引都可以转化为n个点上的索引。概率模型给出召回保证（k_b随n对数增长即可）及匹配的理论极限（投票无法区分低于1/√k_b阶的overlap差异）。该设计的优势在于结构性：构建是n次独立搜索——可完美并行、确定性、在64核上处理10^8向量仅需5,250秒，比匹配召回率的图构建快3.7倍；可在3 GiB强制内存限制下流式构建；便携式工件在8 GB强制预算内从NVMe服务10^8向量，低于SSD图基线的运行下限。代价是查询时间：饱和图基线在RAM中回答查询快6-16倍，0.99召回率的验证预算随n^{0.30}增长。所有结果附带种子、饱和扫描和完整配置，由运行清单生成，包括实测的负面结果。目标应用场景重视构建成本、确定性、内存占用或黑盒度量而非峰值吞吐量：频繁重建的语料库、批量相似性工作负载、内存受限的服务环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有近似最近邻搜索（ANNS）索引在通用度量空间中的可扩展性、可并行性、确定性和内存效率方面存在不足。NAPP索引仅使用常数个枢轴，限制了词汇表规模和召回能力。作者希望设计一种能够利用线性规模词汇表的索引结构，同时保持恒定期望长度的倒排列表，并具备确定性的可并行构建过程，以支持大规模、内存受限和黑盒度量的应用场景。

Method: misi索引的核心构造为：(1) 从数据库中随机采样αn个点作为词汇表；(2) 每个对象通过可插拔的内层索引找到其k_b个最近样本点并加入对应倒排列表，使倒排列表期望长度保持常数ρ=k_b/α；(3) 查询时基于idf加权的共享邻居投票，选出C个候选后精确验证。理论方面，构建了概率模型证明k_b只需随n对数增长即可保证召回率，并证明了投票方法存在1/√k_b阶的分辨率极限。

Result: 在64核上对10^8向量构建索引仅需5,250秒，比匹配召回率的图构建快3.7倍；可在3 GiB内存限制下流式构建；在8 GB强制预算内从NVMe服务10^8向量（低于SSD图基线工作下限）；查询时图基线快6-16倍；0.99召回率的验证预算按n^{0.30}增长。所有实验附带完整配置和可复现性保障，包括负面结果。

Conclusion: misi以查询时间为代价换取了构建速度、确定性、内存效率和黑盒度量适用性等结构性优势，适合频繁重建的语料库、批量相似性负载和内存受限场景。其理论保证（对数级k_b需求）与极限（1/√k_b分辨率下限）为理解共享邻居投票方法在ANNS中的能力边界提供了清晰刻画。

Abstract: We present misi, an inverted index for approximate nearest-neighbor search over general metric spaces whose vocabulary is a random sample of the database, of size proportional to $n$. Each object is represented by its $k_b$ nearest sample points, found by a pluggable inner index over the sample; queries are answered by an idf-weighted shared-neighbor vote followed by exact verification of $C$ candidates. The construction generalizes the NAPP index from a constant number of pivots to a linear-size vocabulary, which keeps posting lists at constant expected length $ρ= k_b/α$ as $n$ grows and turns the index into a combinator: any high-recall index on $αn$ points yields an index on $n$ points, for any metric. A probabilistic model gives a recall guarantee -- $k_b$ logarithmic in $n$ over the overlap gap suffices, with a verification budget the index itself estimates -- and a matching limit: the vote cannot resolve overlap differences below order $1/\sqrt{k_b}$. The design's strengths are structural: construction is $n$ independent searches -- embarrassingly parallel, deterministic, $5{,}250$ s for $10^8$ vectors on 64 cores, $3.7\times$ faster than a matched-recall graph build -- it streams under an enforced 3 GiB cap, and the portable artifact serves $10^8$ vectors from NVMe within an enforced 8 GB budget, below the working floor of the SSD-graph baseline. Its cost is query-time work: saturated graph baselines answer $6$-$16\times$ faster in RAM, and the verification budget for 0.99 recall grows as $n^{0.30}$. All results carry seeds, saturation sweeps and full configurations, are generated from run manifests, and include measured negative results. The intended applications weight construction cost, determinism, memory footprint, or black-box metrics over peak throughput: frequently rebuilt corpora, batch similarity workloads, constrained-memory serving.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [112] [SLM-Conditioned Hierarchical Relation Routing for Labeled Property Graph Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26132)
*Michal Podstawski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出SLM-Conditioned Hierarchical Relation Routing架构，将小型语言模型直接集成到图消息选择中，通过目标条件化的路由查询在关系类型内和跨关系类型两个层级上筛选语义消息，同时保留拓扑GNN的稳定结构表示，实现结构证据与语义信息的融合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统图神经网络通常将节点和关系的异构文本与类别属性编码为静态特征向量，无法根据特定预测目标动态决定哪些语义证据应影响消息传播过程，限制了图学习中对语义信息的利用能力。

Method: 该架构包含：(1) 拓扑GNN提供稳定的结构表示和预测锚点；(2) 对每个目标节点，将邻居的结构状态、节点属性编码、关系属性编码和关系类型组合成入射消息；(3) 参数高效的SLM处理结构化图软标记，生成目标条件化的路由查询；(4) 该查询先在每个关系类型内选择相关消息，再跨关系级摘要进行路由；(5) 最终表示作为拓扑锚点的有界残差更新，既保留结构证据又允许语义信息修改预测。

Result: 该架构支持在邻居级别和关系类型级别进行可解释分析，为将语言派生语义整合到属性丰富的图学习中提供了通用机制。

Conclusion: 通过两层级的分层路由机制，SLM能够理解目标任务上下文，精准选择语义相关的消息，同时以有界残差方式更新拓扑表示，在保持结构学习稳定性的前提下引入语言语义，实现了可解释、可泛化的属性图学习框架。

Abstract: Labeled property graphs combine relational structure with heterogeneous textual and categorical properties attached to both nodes and relationships. Conventional graph neural networks typically represent these properties as static feature vectors, limiting their ability to determine which semantic evidence should influence message propagation for a particular prediction target. We propose SLM-Conditioned Hierarchical Relation Routing, an architecture that integrates a small language model directly into graph message selection. A topology GNN provides a stable structural representation and prediction anchor. For each target node, incident messages combine the neighbor's structural state, node-property encoding, relationship-property encoding, and relationship type. A parameter-efficient SLM processes structured graph soft tokens and produces a target-conditioned routing query. This query first selects relevant messages within each relationship type and subsequently routes information across relation-level summaries. The resulting representation provides a bounded residual update to the topology anchor, preserving structural evidence while allowing contextual semantic information to modify the prediction. The architecture supports interpretable analysis at both the neighbor and relationship-type levels and provides a general mechanism for integrating language-derived semantics into property-rich graph learning.

</details>


### [113] [High Probability Derivative Bounds for Random tanh Neural Networks on a Hypercube](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26526)
*Josef Dick,Michael Feischl,Fabian Zehetgruber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对宽随机神经网络，建立了混合输入导数的高概率界，特别关注tanh网络和Xavier初始化的情形。研究表明，通过隔离最高阶导数的线性项并利用可测有限网控制切向方向，可以将导数界从指数增长（随深度）改善为多项式增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机神经网络在深度增加时，其输入导数的确定性分析（基于权重矩阵的欧几里得算子范数）通常会产生随深度指数增长的界。这种指数增长严重限制了网络正则性分析以及基于正则性（如拟蒙特卡洛积分）的训练方法的理论保证，因此需要寻找更紧的导数界。

Method: 采用概率性分析框架：对于宽的高斯网络，隔离混合导数中最高阶导数的线性项，通过构造可测有限网（measurable finite nets）来控制对应的切向方向，从而避免纯确定性算子范数分析导致的指数增长。激活函数要求其导数满足阶乘增长界，对tanh网络结合Xavier初始化进行具体化分析。

Result: 对于标量输出tanh网络（高斯权重、Xavier初始化），当隐藏宽度满足n ≥ C(L³n₀²(1+log n₀)+L²(1+log(L/η)))时，以至少1-η的概率，混合导数界|D^u R_{Φ(L)}(x)| ≤ C₀|u|!(C₁L)^(|u|-1)∏_{j∈u}β_j(η,n₀)同时对所有非空u和x∈[0,1]^{n₀}成立。一阶导数界与深度无关，m阶方形自由混合导数至多以多项式阶L^(m-1)增长。由此获得网络实现欧几里得Lipschitz常数和加权Sobolev范数的高概率界。

Conclusion: 通过隔离线性项和有限网控制切向方向的技术，宽随机tanh网络的输入导数获得了显著优于指数界的多项式增长界。这些Sobolev范数界为将网络正则性引入拟蒙特卡洛（QMC）积分及其训练分析提供了理论桥梁。

Abstract: We establish high-probability bounds for mixed input derivatives of wide random neural networks whose activation derivatives satisfy a factorial growth bound. Our main result specializes these estimates to $\tanh$ networks with Xavier initialization. A direct deterministic analysis based on Euclidean operator norms of the weight matrices yields derivative bounds that generally grow exponentially with the depth. We show that this growth can be substantially improved for sufficiently wide Gaussian networks by isolating the term that is linear in the highest-order derivative and controlling the corresponding tangent directions by measurable finite nets.
  For scalar-output $\tanh$ networks with Gaussian weights and Xavier initialization, we prove that there exist constants $C,C_0,C_1>0$ such that, whenever the common hidden width satisfies $n \geq C\left(L^3n_0^2(1+\log n_0)+L^2\left(1+\log(L/η)\right)\right)$, then, with probability at least $1-η$, the estimate $\left|D^u\mathcal{R}_{Φ^{(L)}}(x)\right| \leq C_0 |u|! (C_1L)^{|u|-1}\prod_{j\in u}β_j(η,n_0)$ holds simultaneously for every non-empty $u\subseteq[n_0]$ and every $x\in[0,1]^{n_0}$. Thus, the first-order derivative bound is independent of the depth, while a square-free mixed derivative of order $|u|$ grows at most polynomially as $L^{|u|-1}$, apart from the coordinate factors. As consequences, we obtain high-probability bounds for the Euclidean Lipschitz constant and for weighted Sobolev norms of the network realization. The latter connect the derivative estimates to quasi-Monte Carlo integration and indicate how such regularity can enter the analysis of QMC-based training.

</details>


### [114] [NeuronFuzz: Safety Neuron Guided Fuzzing for LLM Safety Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26222)
*Zhiyuan Xu,Muhammad Firhard Roslan,Joseph Gardiner,Sana Belguith,Lichao Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NeuronFuzz是一个白盒模糊测试框架，利用LLM内部安全神经元的激活信号作为连续执行反馈来评估模型安全性，无需生成目标模型响应，显著降低了评估成本并提高了对强对齐模型的越狱攻击发现效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自动化LLM安全测试方法依赖响应级反馈，每个候选提示都需要目标模型生成响应来评估攻击效果，这不仅成本高昂，而且在强对齐模型上提供的信息极为稀疏——大多数候选都被相同的失败结果拒绝，导致难以有效引导搜索。

Method: NeuronFuzz包含两个核心组件：(1) SafetyOracle——通过模板不变的有害/良性输入和稳定性感知选择，识别一组紧凑的安全神经元，并将其激活转换为连续的safety alarm分数，该分数可在prefill阶段获得，无需生成响应；(2) 基于梯度的变异策略——利用safety alarm分数的可微性，识别安全敏感的模板位置，并使用掩码语言模型生成流畅且上下文兼容的变异，同时保留原始有害载荷并避免额外优化变量。

Result: 在21个文本和多模态模型上评估：在5个白盒源模型上实现76-100%的越狱发现率，比基线方法高出最多48个百分点；优化模板零样本迁移到开源权重和6个专有目标模型，平均ASR和top-5集成ASR（EASR）分别达到69.6%/92.6%和44.1%/60.0%。

Conclusion: NeuronFuzz通过利用内部安全神经网络激活作为连续、可微的反馈信号，有效解决了传统响应级反馈成本高、指导稀疏的问题，在跨模型的越狱攻击迁移方面表现出色，为LLM安全性评估提供了一种高效的新范式。

Abstract: Safety evaluation is critical for assessing whether aligned Large Language Models (LLMs) remain robust against jailbreak attacks. Existing automated testing methods, however, largely rely on response-level feedback: each candidate prompt typically requires generating a target-model response to evaluate its attack effectiveness. This process is expensive and, more importantly, provides only sparse guidance on strongly aligned models, where most candidates are rejected with the same failure outcome.
  This paper presents NeuronFuzz, a white-box fuzzing framework that exploits internal safety neurons as continuous execution feedback for LLM safety evaluation. A SafetyOracle converts safety-neuron activations into a continuous safety alarm score that serves as feedback for fuzzing and can be obtained during prefill, eliminating response generation from the fuzzing loop. To construct the SafetyOracle, NeuronFuzz uses template-invariant harmful and benign inputs and stability-aware selection to identify a compact set of safety neurons whose activations capture harmful-intent recognition. Moreover, since the safety alarm score is differentiable, NeuronFuzz uses its gradients to identify safety-sensitive template positions and a masked language model to generate fluent, context-compatible mutations while preserving original harmful payload and avoiding additional optimization variables.
